### §2·R4a — the four PASS-4 escapes, disposed AGAINST THE CANONICAL and never against the check

**The disposition rule, stated once (R4-10).** An escape is answered by BOOKING when the canonical homes the id at F.W1, and by CITING ITS HOLDER when it does not — because *"an id booked that the canonical does not home there is that wave's fabrication"*, and a wave that cures an escape by inventing a row has traded a MINOR for a fabrication. Two of the four are canonical F.W1 rows and are BOOKED; two are not, and are CITED at the holders R4-6 named.

**AND THE RULE IS RETROACTIVE (repair round 5, W1-43).** It was written for the four PASS-4 escapes and it was never run over §2·R1, §2·R2 or §2·R3 — so the file simultaneously legislated the test and exempted the three rounds that most needed it. **It is now run over all of them, in both directions.** Forward: §2·R5 converts **nine legs** from bookings to citations (`fr-Tooltip FR-TT-24` · `fr-SliderControl R-14` · `fr-EquationView M-TL` · `fr-EquationView D·D-M3` · `fr-CanvasOverlayButton FR-COB-28` · `fr-CoefficientsPanel FR-CP-33` · `fr-BasisSelector m-6` · `fr-CollapsibleSection m-7` · `fr-NotationPills FR-NP-6`) and **four non-debts** (`fr-InfoCard FR-IC-17`, canonical TERMINAL; `fr-PaperSearch D-14 · D-15 · D-17`, canonical UNROUTED). Backward: **§6·R5 books the twenty-three the sweep had missed in the other direction** — ids the canonical DOES home at F.W1 and this spec neither booked nor cited. **A law applied forward and never backward is a law that has not landed**, and the two directions are one act: the same rule that forbids claiming a row you do not hold requires holding the rows you are given.

### §2·R5c — what this round did NOT do

**No id is re-graded, re-named or re-homed by this spec.** The four rows that left the F.W1 roster left it at the CENSUS, by dated errata this file consumes and does not author (E5-3 · E5-5 · E5-6 · E5-17). **No detector is re-cut** (R4-3): the roster at §6·R4 is the canonical's paste, verified byte-identical by `diff` and not by eye. **No gate is green-stamped**: G20 leg (b) remains born-RED and is a non-author seat's to run, and §6·R4a's `grep -F` probe is expressly RETIRED as a closure instrument this round because it can no longer fail. **No product source is opened.** **No sibling spec is written** — the nine holders above are cited by §-heading, gate id and row label, never by a line number into a live file (R2-2).

### §6·R4 — THE CANONICAL F.W1 ROSTER (**330 rows, 65 records** ⟨*chased at round-7 reconcile, 2026-08-29: read 358; re-based on `CENSUS-CANONICAL.md` §0.5 errata **E6-3** — 28 killed-claims rows → TERMINAL*⟩ — re-based at repair round 5 on `CENSUS-CANONICAL.md` §0.4 errata E5-3 · E5-5 · E5-6 · E5-17), BOOKED OR CITED — the operand of record for G20 leg (b)

**What this section is, and what it replaces (rulings R4-10 + R4-3.4).** Four conformance passes each re-invented the detector and each returned a different denominator (357 → 404 → 430, with a disclosed narrow reading at 341), which is not four measurements of one thing but four things. `conformance/CENSUS-CANONICAL.md` ends that by fixing the derivation ONCE, and R4-10 makes its §2 roster **this wave's record-side obligation, verbatim and entire**. Below is that roster, pasted whole, with its reproduction command; then the disposition ledger. **This spec derives no roster of its own, samples nothing, and re-cuts no detector** — and it green-stamps nothing: the record-qualified membership run is G20 leg (b)'s, executed by a seat that did not author this file (R4-4's two-key falsifier).

#### §6·R4a — the membership probe, its SHAPE, and its honest limit

**Run against the PRE-REPAIR bytes of this file (sha256 `83ffe83cb37f`), disclosed as a DATED PRIOR RUN and not re-pasted as a live figure** — because a self-descriptive membership figure inside a file the same round is still editing is exactly the shape PASS-3 D-2 convicted and R3-3.10 killed. The probe, published so a non-author seat can run it at the settled bytes:

`R=docs/tranches/X/fourier` — then, for each id yielded by the roster command above: `grep -Fq -- "$id" "$R"/waves/F-W1.md` ⇒ **SPEC**; else `grep -Fq -- "$id" "$R"/carry/F-W1-CARRY.md` ⇒ **CARRY**; else **NEITHER**. At `83ffe83cb37f` the partition was **SPEC 315 · CARRY 11 · NEITHER 36**; the 47 non-SPEC ids are adjudicated one by one at §6·R4b and §6·R4c below, and every one of them is now named in this file, so a re-run at the settled bytes will not reproduce that partition and is not meant to.

⊘ **RETIRED AS A CLOSURE INSTRUMENT AT REPAIR ROUND 5 (PASS-5 P5-5's successor defect).** The disclosure above is retained verbatim and the probe is retained as a PRESENCE test; what is withdrawn is any reading of it as evidence of closure. Re-run at the settled bytes by this seat, with the pasted roster excluded so the paste cannot green itself — ⟨cmd⟩ (`$X`) `awk 'NR<550 \|\| NR>614' waves/F-W1.md > /tmp/specnr.txt`, then the three-way partition above against `/tmp/specnr.txt` and `carry/F-W1-CARRY.md` — the probe returns **SPEC 330 · CARRY 0 · NEITHER 0** ⟨*re-based at round-7 reconcile, 2026-08-29: read 358*⟩. **It greens every id in the roster, including ids with zero word-boundary occurrences anywhere in this file**, because §6·R4b, §6·R4c and §6·R5 now name them all in prose. **A test that cannot fail is not a test**, and a partition that reports 330/0/0 is reporting on this file's vocabulary, not on its bookings.

**The only admissible instrument is G20 leg (b)'s RECORD-QUALIFIED join, run by a non-author seat, whose LHS is §6·R4's paste and whose test is the `(record, id)` PAIR.** The reason is the limit disclosed below and it is now measured rather than asserted: `grep -F` over a bare token cannot distinguish `fr-GlassTimeline C-18` from `C-18.1`/`C-18.2`, `fr-AnimationControls R-14` from `fr-SliderControl R-14`, or `fr-AdminFlaggedPanel K1` from the `K1` token in thirteen other records — **and each of those three collisions hid a real escape for four passes.** **That join's first work is the homonym tail PASS-5 declared but did not count** — `R-1` ×3 · `R-5` ×4 · `K-8` ×4 · `K-9` ×2 · `C-4` ×4 · `C-6` ×2 · `C-11` ×4 · `K-5` ×3 · `F-1` ×2 · `F-3` ×2, ~15 pairs — **and resolving it re-grades no record and re-homes no id**: it decides only which of two same-spelled rows a given cell was always talking about.

**THE PROBE'S LIMIT, DISCLOSED RATHER THAN LAUNDERED — this is a presence test, never a closure certificate.** The probe is `grep -F` over bare id tokens, and the canonical's own §0.1 warns that a bare short token is not an identity; this spec's §6·R1 homonym list is the same warning from the other end. Where a roster id is a short token — `C-4` · `K-1` · `R-5` · `C-11` · `C-13` · `M-7` · `L-7` · `D-1` · `K-8` and their kin recur across many records — a SPEC hit proves the *bytes are present* and does **not** prove the **(record, id) pair** is booked. **The record-qualified test is G20 leg (b)'s, its LHS is the roster above, and it is run by a seat that did not write this file.** This file states the operand and the shape; it does not certify its own closure, and the four passes' history is the reason that sentence is load-bearing.

### §E-3·R2 — dated errata, repair round 2 (corrections travel as rows; no dated artefact is rewritten)

**Scope note.** Round 2's corrections divide in two, and the division is the discipline. Corrections that would have SHIFTED bytes land as new rows (§2·R2 above). Corrections that are **within-line** — a struck coordinate, a clipped quote, a wrong count word — land in place at the cell that carries the error, because a stale figure left standing beside a correction row is a second defect; each such in-place edit is minuted here so that none of them is a defect's silent patch. Nothing below rewrites a dated *artefact*: the CARRY, the 66 records, `conformance/PASS-1/*` and `conformance/PASS-2/*` are untouched.

---

## §7 Archaeology (why the guardrails have these shapes)

The prior attempt is the **uncommitted `m/w1-bump-migration` working tree** — the 28 dirty paths every "at the pin" statement in the 66-record corpus actually describes (FR-EQR-33(c)/GAB-13). Its failure mode is the one this wave is built against: a bump was begun; a rename sweep ran over a **variant string** instead of a **token family** (3-of-4 literals, 0-of-4 declarations — B-2's mechanism); the lock was regenerated and dropped a required peer (FR-EQC-7); and the whole thing sat un-landed for months while five audits measured it as if it were the pin. Guardrails: G3 lands or explicitly baselines the tree before any budget commits; G9 makes acceptance a token-family grep; G13 makes the lock reproducible; G6 makes the transaction indivisible so a half-landed bump cannot exist again.
